### Summary Generation for Amazon Product Review

To generate a summary of a review, I'll need to process the text and extract the key points. Here's a Python code snippet that uses the Natural Language Processing (NLP) library NLTK to perform this task:

```python
import nltk
from nltk.sentiment import SentimentIntensityAnalyzer
from nltk.corpus
-----
-----
1578
**Summary:** Buyer received the travel size product instead of the 8.1 oz product, despite being charged for the larger size.
